Directory Structure

Collapsible directory tree showing file and symbol counts. Directories with LLM summaries show them inline.

java/5 files · 20 symbols
This directory contains a suite of Java CLI tools for processing BMDExpress 3 project files (.bm2 format), enabling data extraction, format conversion, and statistical analysis in a unified workflow. The tools support exporting genomics and category-level analysis to JSON and TSV, integrating multiple project files, performing prefilter statistical tests (Williams trend test, Dunnett's comparisons), and round-tripping between JSON and native .bm2 formats. Together, they form a data pipeline for dose-response genomics analysis and metadata enrichment.
output/1 files · 1 symbols
This directory contains a utility for generating documentation about the BMDExpress dose-response gene expression analysis pipeline. It uses python-docx to create a formatted Word document (.docx) that explains the interpretation pipeline, including structured headings, paragraphs, tables, and styled text.
pdf_text/3 files · 51 symbols
pdf_text is a from-scratch PDF parser library designed to extract semantic text from PDFs while replacing external PDF dependencies (requiring only zlib). It handles the complete PDF processing pipeline—resolving object graphs, decompressing streams, walking page trees, classifying text by semantic structure, and supporting advanced features like ToUnicode CMaps and character encodings. The package also includes a lossless PDF codec that can decompose PDFs into labeled byte spans for JSON storage and perfectly reconstruct them.
tests/21 files · 272 symbols
The `tests` directory contains the test suite for the project, with pytest configuration and shared fixtures in `conftest.py` that provide test isolation, state management, and mocking for external dependencies such as Java subprocesses, HTTP calls, and LLM APIs. This setup ensures tests run in clean, isolated environments without cross-test contamination.
e2e/4 files · 18 symbols
This directory contains end-to-end tests for a data pipeline system using Pytest and Playwright, with support for visual step-through debugging, screenshots, and structured logging. The tests validate the complete pipeline flow from session loading or data upload through validation, integration, processing, and approval stages using both headless smoke tests and interactive headful browser tests. The test infrastructure provides fixtures for live test server deployment and interactive test inspection capabilities.
fixtures/1 files · 0 symbols
The tests/fixtures directory serves as a Python package for organizing test fixtures and supporting data used across the project's test suite. Currently, it contains only an `__init__.py` file marking it as a package, with fixture modules likely to be added as needed.
integration/6 files · 45 symbols
The `tests/integration` directory contains integration tests for a biomedical data management system, covering file uploads, session lifecycle management (load/approve/unapprove), pool validation and integration endpoints, and complex data processing workflows that generate section cards with tables and summaries across multiple platforms. These tests validate HTTP responses, in-memory caching, domain model correctness (platform names, required JSON fields), and error handling for key API endpoints.
unit/8 files · 198 symbols
The tests/unit directory contains comprehensive unit tests for the project's core modules, covering document tree manipulation, gene expression data filtering and platform-based partitioning, utility functions for dose-response experiments, and session persistence mechanisms. These tests verify the correctness of data structures, transformation logic, and file I/O operations across the codebase.

File Inventory

FileLanguageLinesSymbols
abstract_apical.pypython4757
Generates deterministic prose narratives summarizing apical endpoint BMD (benchmark dose) findings for toxicology reports, including per-platform and per-sex analyses with reliable BMD estimates, UREP flags, and LOEL-only endpoints.
abstract_genomics.pypython2753
Builds the genomics portion of an Abstract's Results paragraph by converting gene sets and individual gene data into formatted natural language sentences describing the most sensitive biological processes and genes by organ and sex.
abstract_methods.pypython1561
Deterministic template-style generator for the Abstract → Methods paragraph in scientific reports, pulling all facts from MethodsContext without requiring LLM calls.
abstract_pk.pypython1554
Generates a narrative paragraph comparing pharmacokinetic data (plasma concentrations and half-lives) between male and female rats across dose groups and timepoints for inclusion in scientific abstracts.
abstract_summary.pypython3155
Builds Abstract Summary and Results paragraphs by aggregating the lowest reliable BMDs across apical endpoints and genomics data (gene sets, individual genes) for each sex, formatted per NIEHS Report 10 standards.
apical_bmds.pypython3294
Implements EPA BMDS continuous modeling via pybmds for apical endpoint dose-response data, providing BMD/BMDL/BMDU estimates with NIEHS classification and supporting both single and batch parallel processing.
background_server.pypython4208
FastAPI application entry point for 5dToxReport that wires together endpoint routers, applies middleware (user-gate access control, no-cache dev headers), and serves a single-page web UI with document structure injection.
background_writer.pypython76610
LLM-based background section writer for toxicology studies that assembles regulatory/toxicological data into a structured prompt and generates 6-7 paragraphs of formal scientific prose suitable for rat dose-response study reports, with support for Claude API and local Ollama models.
bmd_project_schema.pypython99319
Pydantic models that validate integrated.json, the canonical merged dataset from bmdx-pipe, enforcing schema conformance at load time while preserving unknown fields. Includes domain invariants for deduplicating legacy apical experiments and repointing BMD results to truth siblings.
body_weight_table.pypython6983
Builds NIEHS Table 2 (Body Weights) directly from sidecar JSON files containing raw animal-level data, computing mean±SE from Core Animals only and generating attrition footnotes for animals that died before study completion.
build_db.pypython40810
Builds a normalized DuckDB analytical database by loading and deduplicating papers, genes, GO terms, pathways, and citation networks from crawl data across multiple sources.
build_docx.pypython1106
Provides reusable formatting utilities for python-docx Document objects, enabling consistent styling of headings, paragraphs, bullet lists, and tables for DOCX report generation.
cache_plumbing.pypython44011
Manages per-section disk caching for an expensive processing pipeline, with hash functions to detect input changes and serializers for platform tables and category lookups. Each cache stage invalidates independently based on its actual inputs rather than downstream changes.
chem_resolver.pypython49713
Resolves chemical identifiers (name, CASRN, CID, DTXSID) to comprehensive metadata including properties, synonyms, and functional uses via PubChem PUG REST and EPA CompTox APIs.
citegraph.pypython88328
Citation graph crawler that uses the Semantic Scholar API to expand a graph from seed papers, scoring relevance and stopping when graph saturation or resource budgets are exhausted.
clinical_pathology_table.pypython3961
Builds NIEHS Tables 4/5/6 (Clinical Chemistry, Hematology, Hormones) from sidecar animal-level data and NTP statistical analysis, producing combined Male/Female tables with endpoint rows, n-counts, mean±SE values, significance markers, and BMD/BMDL columns for responsive endpoints.
crawl_phase2.pypython4703
Coordinates Phase 2 literature crawls across 3 tiers (mechanism-specific, organ-gap, and gene-tox) using configurable search parameters and keyword boosting to fill knowledge gaps identified by gap analysis.
data_gatherer.pypython111319
Gathers regulatory and toxicological data from multiple public databases (PubChem, EPA, ATSDR, NIOSH, Semantic Scholar) to populate structured toxicology background data for chemical risk assessment.
document_tree.pypython57310
Defines the complete document structure for NIEHS biological potency reports as a declarative tree of DocNode objects, serving as the single source of truth for section hierarchy, table/figure numbering, and navigation. Provides utilities to traverse, query, and serialize the tree for use in Typst templates and frontend TOC.
enrichr_analysis.pypython2173
CLI tool for running Enrichr enrichment analysis on genes extracted from rlm-bmdx genomics session JSON files; handles argument parsing, session file reading, and result formatting while delegating API calls to enrichr_client.py.
enrichr_client.pypython2143
Minimal client for the Enrichr gene set enrichment analysis REST API, providing functions to submit gene lists and fetch enrichment results from biological databases.
export_routes.pypython3585
FastAPI router providing endpoints for exporting 5dToxReport documents as tagged PDF/UA-1 and enriched .bm2 files, plus managing global writing style profiles.
export_sessions.pypython2366
Exports Claude Code session JSONL transcripts to readable Markdown documents, parsing and formatting user messages, assistant responses, and tool interactions with collapsible sections for large outputs.
extract.pypython109319
LLM-based extraction system that uses local Ollama instances to extract structured information (claims, genes, organs, methods, species) from scientific paper abstracts and full texts, processing papers in parallel across multiple GPU endpoints and performing gene consensus analysis.
extract_phase2_runner.pypython280
Batch runner script that executes Phase 2 extraction across 9 crawl directories using ParallelExtractionEngine, processing papers from JSON files and saving results with elapsed time tracking.
fulltext.pypython57222
Fetches full paper text from multiple open-access and institutional sources (PMC, arXiv, S2, Unpaywall, DOI proxy) in priority order, with caching and batch processing support.
gene_bodies.pypython4755
Generates narrative prose for genomics analysis reports, building introductory paragraphs and per-organ findings sections for Gene Set BMD Analysis and Gene BMD Analysis with standardized sentence structures following NIEHS Report 10 format.

Implements a second-pass Semantic Scholar crawler that searches for gene-function papers explaining how genes matter in organ-specific biology, with optional toxicology-focused variant for flagged genes.
genomics_narratives.pypython1953

Builds a GO term → gene mapping by merging rat and human gene annotations (GAF files) and joining with reference UMAP data, tagging genes by species and cross-referencing with toxicogenomics consensus evidence.

Package initialization that exports the public API for pdf_text, a from-scratch PDF parser with semantic text extraction, font metadata, and lossless codec. Re-exports core parsing functions, text utilities, and byte-perfect codec operations from internal modules.
pdf_text/parse_pdf.pypython218742
From-scratch PDF parser that reads raw PDF bytes, resolves the object graph, walks the page tree, decompresses content streams, and extracts text with semantic classification using marked-content structure tags. Handles Object Streams, ToUnicode CMaps, and includes post-processing for table detection.
pdf_text/pdf_codec.pypython4309
Lossless PDF decomposer and reassembler that breaks PDFs into labeled byte spans (header, objects, gaps, trailer), stores them in JSON with base64 encoding, and can perfectly reconstruct the original file with SHA-256 verification.
pool_admin.pypython3899
Superuser CLI tool for managing the file pool behind a toxicology report system, enabling listing, deletion, and invalidation of data files and downstream artifacts with dry-run support.
pool_fingerprints.pypython3696
Manages file-pool fingerprinting and lightweight validation by extracting structural metadata from uploaded files, caching fingerprints to disk, and validating new uploads against the existing pool, with automatic long-format to wide-format conversion.
pool_globals.pypython1435
Holds shared mutable state (fingerprints, integrated pool, data uploads), a FastAPI router, and path helpers for the pool-orchestrator subsystem across split modules, with accessor functions and lazy imports to break circular dependencies.
pool_orchestrator.pypython1860
A backward-compatible facade module that re-exports the public API from split submodules (pool_globals, pool_state, section_serializers, etc.), allowing existing consumers to continue importing via `from pool_orchestrator import …` without code churn. Also triggers route registration via side-effect imports.
pool_routes.pypython4805
FastAPI route handlers for the file-pool lifecycle that manage the upload → validate → confirm-metadata → integrate workflow, providing four POST endpoints and a private metadata-writing helper to move validation reports, precedence decisions, metadata assignments, and final pool integration through the UI.
pool_state.pypython2023
Handles pool-state mutations: checks if a pool has progressed past the uploaded phase, removes stale file entries on re-upload, and invalidates downstream artifacts while surgically preserving content-hash-keyed caches and user-edited narratives.
process_integrated.pypython9567
FastAPI endpoints for orchestrating integrated BMDProject processing (NTP stats, BMDS modeling, genomics, LLM narratives) and generating per-animal traceability reports.
processing_helpers.pypython10138
Helper functions for a multi-stage pipeline that processes integrated BMD (Benchmark Dose) projects: filters gene expression, partitions data by platform, builds UI section cards with specialized table builders, extracts genomics and adversity signatures, and generates BMD summaries.
report_pdf.pypython170315
Builds PDF/UA-1 compliant NIEHS reports from structured data by compiling Typst templates, handling scaffolding, overlaying real content, filtering sections, and managing footnotes.

session_routes.pypython158016
FastAPI endpoints for session persistence, section approval/history management, and experiment metadata editing. Handles loading/saving approved report sections (background, BMD summary, genomics, methods), version history, and destructive resets.
